Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The waves variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 224 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 362 and intersection form of rank 2.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to waves theory, we decompose the problem into 224 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 362. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.
